--- A98/A99: codex Stop stdin, gate reads the rollout named by transcript_path\n  New-Item $tmp -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n  $sidCx  = \"selftest-a99-$PID\"\n  $flagCx = Join-Path $HOME \".claude\\handoff-watch\\$sidCx\"\n  function CxStop($tp) {\n    if (-not $tp) { return \"{`\"session_id`\":`\"$sidCx`\",`\"stop_hook_active`\":false}\" }\n    $tpEsc = $tp.Replace('\\', '\\\\')   # raw JSON bytes: one path separator -> two backslash chars\n    \"{`\"session_id`\":`\"$sidCx`\",`\"transcript_path`\":`\"$tpEsc`\",`\"stop_hook_active`\":false}\"\n  }\n  function ClearCx { Remove-Item \"$flagCx*\" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }\n\n  # 1. transcript_path is a rollout -> gate reads it (no window_minutes: the\n  #    ordinary 92% gate applies).\n  ClearCx\n  $rp1 = AddCodex $tmp 95.0 12000 200000 0 'a99-read'\n  $r = Gate (CxStop $rp1)\n  if ($r -notmatch '\"decision\":\"block\"' -or $r -notmatch '95') { throw \"gate: did not read codex rollout via transcript_path, got '$r'\" }\n  ClearCx\n\n  # 2. transcript_path NOT named rollout-*.jsonl -> ignored, even though the\n  #    file content alone would fire.\n  $notRollDir = Join-Path $tmp 'a99-notroll'\n  New-Item $notRollDir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n  $notRoll = Join-Path $notRollDir 'session-notroll.jsonl'\n  Copy-Item $rp1 $notRoll -Force\n  $r = Gate (CxStop $notRoll)\n  if ($r) { throw \"gate: fired on a transcript_path that is not rollout-*.jsonl, got '$r'\" }\n  ClearCx\n\n  # 3. single used_percent value (the ps1 scalar `+=` bug, A83): only a primary\n  #    window, no secondary. max(50, 45) = 50, under 92 - the buggy scalar sum\n  #    (50+45=95) would fire.\n  $rp3 = AddCodex $tmp 50.0 90000 200000 0 'a99-scalar' $null -NoSecondary\n  $r = Gate (CxStop $rp3)\n  if ($r) { throw \"gate: single used_percent scalar bug regressed (50+45 would fire), got '$r'\" }\n  ClearCx\n\n  # 4. 10080-minute window at 91% -> the weekly gate fires (A98).\n  $rp4 = AddCodex $tmp 91.0 12000 200000 0 'a99-weekly' 10080\n  $r = Gate (CxStop $rp4)\n  if ($r -notmatch '\"decision\":\"block\"' -or $r -notmatch 'AskUserQuestion') { throw \"gate: codex 10080-minute window at 91% did not fire the weekly order, got '$r'\" }\n  ClearCx\n\n  # 5. 300-minute window at 91% -> silent (below the 92% ordinary threshold).\n  $rp5 = AddCodex $tmp 91.0 12000 200000 0 'a99-session' 300\n  $r = Gate (CxStop $rp5)\n  if ($r) { throw \"gate: codex 300-minute window at 91% fired (should need 92%), got '$r'\" }\n  ClearCx\n\n  # The sh twin must agree on all five.\n  if ($shExe) {\n    $hSh = NewHome 'a99-sh'\n    $flagCxSh = Join-Path $hSh \".claude\\handoff-watch\\$sidCx\"\n    function ClearCxSh { Remove-Item \"$flagCxSh*\" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue }\n\n    $g = RunSh 'gate' $hSh (CxStop $rp1)\n    if ($g -notmatch '\"decision\":\"block\"' -or $g -notmatch '95') { throw \"sh gate: did not read codex rollout via transcript_path, got '$g'\" }\n    ClearCxSh\n\n    $g = RunSh 'gate' $hSh (CxStop $notRoll)\n    if ($g) { throw \"sh gate: fired on a transcript_path that is not rollout-*.jsonl, got '$g'\" }\n    ClearCxSh\n\n    $g = RunSh 'gate' $hSh (CxStop $rp3)\n    if ($g) { throw \"sh gate: single used_percent scalar bug regressed, got '$g'\" }\n    ClearCxSh\n\n    $g = RunSh 'gate' $hSh (CxStop $rp4)\n    if ($g -notmatch '\"decision\":\"block\"' -or $g -notmatch 'AskUserQuestion') { throw \"sh gate: codex 10080-minute window at 91% did not fire the weekly order, got '$g'\" }\n    ClearCxSh\n\n    $g = RunSh 'gate' $hSh (CxStop $rp5)\n    if ($g) { throw \"sh gate: codex 300-minute window at 91% fired (should need 92%), got '$g'\" }\n    ClearCxSh\n  }\n\n  'ok'\n}\nfinally {\n  # try/finally, not a trailing line: a failing assertion must still clean up.\n  Remove-Item \"$flag\", \"$flag.weekly\", \"$flag.done\", \"$flag.weekly.done\" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n  Remove-Item (Join-Path $HOME \".claude\\handoff-watch\\selftest-a99-$PID*\") -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n  Remove-Item $tmp -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue\n}\n\r\n### C:\\Users\\Ikran\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\claude\\C--tools-Projects-skillator2\\513fec64-069a-4a3e-839f-33d114b30f8f\\scratchpad\\a58d\\audit-sherlock-2\\.agents\\skills\\watch-cortana\\hooks\\usage-watch.ps1\r\n# watch-cortana: record usage % and act on it before the session is cut off.\n#   -Mode probe [-Then \"<original statusline command>\"]   (Claude Code statusLine)\n#   -Mode gate                                            (Claude Code / codex Stop hook)\n#   -Mode check                                           (any host, no stdin - see below)\n# Threshold: $env:CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_PCT, default 92.\n#\n# Claude Code and codex (A83: gate reads the rollout named by transcript_path)\n# have a Stop hook that can inject. Cursor/Antigravity get `check`, which reads\n# whatever the host leaves on disk and prints the order for the agent to follow.\n# Wired by the always-on project file grayskull-power writes, not by a hook.\nparam([ValidateSet('probe','gate','check')][string]$Mode = 'probe', [string]$Then)\n\n# The state dir keeps its original name on purpose - see the sh twin.\n$dir      = Join-Path $HOME '.claude/handoff-watch'\n$pctLimit = if ($env:CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_PCT) { [double]$env:CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_PCT } else { 92 }\n# The 7-day window is watched separately and lower. The 5-hour window refills\n# in hours, so crossing it is a pause; the weekly one does not, so crossing it\n# ends the week's work - which is why it gets its own threshold and its own\n# fourth step: ask where to go next rather than leave the user to guess.\n$wkLimit  = if ($env:CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_WEEKLY_PCT) { [double]$env:CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_WEEKLY_PCT } else { 90 }\n$inv      = [Globalization.CultureInfo]::InvariantCulture\n# A32: a rollout nobody has written to in this long is not this session's usage.\n# Must match $stale_secs in the sh twin (10800).\n$staleHours = 3\n\n# The flag file is shared between this script and its sh twin, so it must be\n# bare bytes: no BOM, no trailing CRLF, invariant decimal point. Set-Content\n# -Encoding utf8 on PS 5.1 writes \"EF BB BF 31 32 2E 30 0D 0A\", and the sh\n# reader's awk then string-compares 0xEF against the threshold and fires at 12%.\nfunction Write-Flag($path, $value) {\n  $dirPart = Split-Path $path -Parent\n  New-Item $dirPart -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n  # WriteAllText resolves relative to the process cwd, not PowerShell's - be absolute.\n  $abs = [IO.Path]::Combine((Convert-Path $dirPart), (Split-Path $path -Leaf))\n  [IO.File]::WriteAllText($abs, ([double]$value).ToString($inv))\n}\n\n# Read a percentage defensively: first numeric token in the file, so a BOM, a\n# CR, stray whitespace or a half-written line can never become a bogus number.\nfunction Read-Flag($path) {\n  if (-not (Test-Path $path)) { return $null }\n  try { $t = [IO.File]::ReadAllText((Convert-Path $path)) } catch { return $null }\n  if ($t -match '[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?') { return [double]::Parse($Matches[0], $inv) }\n  return $null\n}\n\n# Step 4 is spliced in BEFORE Get-Reason's closing \"Then tell the user where\n# the file is and stop.\" - appending it after put the stop instruction ahead of\n# the one step whose whole point is that the session must not stop on a\n# summary. The sh twin has always spliced; this is the mirror catching up.\nfunction Get-WeeklyReason($pct, $limit) {\n  $tail = ' Then tell the user where the file is and stop.'\n  $step4 = ' (4) This is the 7-DAY window, which does not refill for days - the work is over for now, not paused. So after the handoff, do not stop on a summary: use AskUserQuestion to put the next direction to the user as concrete options drawn from the open board and the handoff''s own next-steps, say which one you recommend and why in one line, and make the recommendation the first option.'\n  (Get-Reason $pct $limit).Replace($tail, $step4 + $tail)\n}\n\nfunction Get-Reason($pct, $limit) {\n  \"Usage has reached $pct% of the limit (threshold $limit%). Stop the current work and preserve the session now - it can be cut off at any moment. In order: (1) if any subagent, workflow or background task is still running, wait for it or stop it and record what it had done - never leave in-flight agent work undescribed; (2) invoke skillator:tickets-zordon to sync TICKETS.md - sync statuses only, do NOT start working open tickets, usage is nearly gone: close what actually landed, mark what is half-done as in-progress, and file a ticket for anything discovered this session that has no ticket; (3) invoke skillator:handoff-cortana and write the document, whose status table must match TICKETS.md ticket-for-ticket and must list the in-flight agent work from step 1 with the exact prompt needed to resume it. Then tell the user where the file is and stop.\"\n}\n\n# Last token_count line of a codex rollout, or $null.\nfunction Get-CodexLastTc($path) {\n  Get-Content -LiteralPath $path -Tail 400 | Where-Object { $_ -match '\"token_count\"' } | Select-Object -Last 1\n}\n\n# Usage % from one codex rollout: the max of every rate_limits used_percent\n# whose window is NOT the 7-day one (A98: window_minutes 10080), plus the\n# live context on its last token_count line; $null when there is none.\nfunction Get-CodexPct($path) {\n  $line = Get-CodexLastTc $path\n  if (-not $line) { return $null }\n  # @() because a lone used_percent comes back as a scalar, and `+=` on a\n  # scalar double ADDS the context % to it (19.0 + 7.3 = 26.3, A83).\n  $vals = @([regex]::Matches($line, '\\{[^{}]*\"used_percent\"\\s*:\\s*[0-9.]+[^{}]*\\}') |\n    Where-Object { $_.Value -notmatch '\"window_minutes\"\\s*:\\s*10080' } |\n    ForEach-Object { [double]([regex]::Match($_.Value, '\"used_percent\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9.]+)').Groups[1].Value) })\n  # last_token_usage is the size of the live context; total_token_usage is\n  # cumulative for the whole session and would read well over 100%.\n  if ($line -match '\"last_token_usage\"\\s*:\\s*\\{[^}]*?\"total_tokens\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9]+)[^}]*\\}[\\s\\S]*?\"model_context_window\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9]+)') {\n    $vals += [math]::Round(100 * [double]$Matches[1] / [double]$Matches[2], 1)\n  }\n  if ($vals.Count) { return ($vals | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum }\n  return $null\n}\n\n# A98: the 10080-minute rate_limits window is codex's 7-day window, the\n# counterpart of the Claude path's seven_day.used_percentage - it gets the\n# weekly gate (90% hard stop, step-4 question) instead of the 92% one.\n# $null when the last token_count line has no such window.\nfunction Get-CodexWeeklyPct($path) {\n  $line = Get-CodexLastTc $path\n  if (-not $line) { return $null }\n  $vals = @([regex]::Matches($line, '\\{[^{}]*\"used_percent\"\\s*:\\s*[0-9.]+[^{}]*\\}') |\n    Where-Object { $_.Value -match '\"window_minutes\"\\s*:\\s*10080' } |\n    ForEach-Object { [double]([regex]::Match($_.Value, '\"used_percent\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9.]+)').Groups[1].Value) })\n  if ($vals.Count) { return ($vals | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum }\n  return $null\n}\n\nif ($Mode -eq 'check') {\n  # Codex: the rollout JSONL carries rate_limits.*.used_percent and the context total.\n  # CODEX_HOME, same as install.ps1 - a relocated codex home must still be watched.\n  $codexHome = if ($env:CODEX_HOME) { $env:CODEX_HOME } else { Join-Path $HOME '.codex' }\n  $roll = Get-ChildItem (Join-Path $codexHome 'sessions') -Recurse -Filter 'rollout-*.jsonl' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |\n          Sort-Object LastWriteTime | Select-Object -Last 1\n  $pct = $null; $wk = $null; $src = 'none'; $key = 'none'\n  if ($roll -and $roll.LastWriteTime -gt (Get-Date).AddHours(-$staleHours)) {\n    $pct = Get-CodexPct $roll.FullName\n    $wk  = Get-CodexWeeklyPct $roll.FullName\n    if ($null -ne $pct -or $null -ne $wk) { $src = 'codex'; $key = $roll.BaseName }\n  }\n  # Claude Code: the statusline probe already wrote the number.\n  if ($null -eq $pct -and $null -eq $wk) {\n    # `.weekly` is written after the main flag, so it is always the newest -\n    # picking it here would report the 7-day number as the max and compare it\n    # against the wrong threshold. It is read below, by name, not by mtime.\n    $f = Get-ChildItem $dir -File -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |\n         Where-Object { $_.Extension -notin @('.done', '.weekly') } | Sort-Object LastWriteTime | Select-Object -Last 1\n    if ($f) { $pct = Read-Flag $f.FullName; if ($null -ne $pct) { $src = 'claude-code'; $key = $f.Name; $wk = Read-Flag \"$($f.FullName).weekly\" } }\n  }\n  # ponytail: cursor and antigravity expose no usage anywhere on disk - say so\n  # rather than invent a number. Upgrade here if either ever writes one.\n  if ($null -eq $pct) { \"watch-cortana: no usage signal on this host - run skillator:handoff-cortana manually before you run out\"; exit 0 }\n  $done   = Join-Path $dir \"$key.done\"\n  $wkDone = Join-Path $dir \"$key.weekly.done\"\n  # The weekly gate applies on every host, not just the one with a Stop hook,\n  # and has its own .done so a 5-hour fire earlier in the session cannot eat\n  # the weekly order when the 7-day window crosses hours later.\n  if ($null -ne $wk -and $wk -ge $wkLimit -and -not (Test-Path $wkDone)) {\n    New-Item $dir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n    New-Item $wkDone -ItemType File -Force | Out-Null\n    \"HANDOFF NOW ($src 7-day $wk%)\"; Get-WeeklyReason $wk $wkLimit\n  } elseif ($pct -ge $pctLimit -and -not (Test-Path $done)) {\n    New-Item $dir -ItemType Directory -Force | Out-Null\n    New-Item $done -ItemType File -Force | Out-Null\n    \"HANDOFF NOW ($src $pct%)\"; Get-Reason $pct $pctLimit\n  } else {\n    \"watch-cortana: $src $pct% of $pctLimit% - ok\"\n  }\n  exit 0\n}\n\n$raw  = [Console]::In.ReadToEnd()\n$sid  = if ($raw -match '\"session_id\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"') { $Matches[1] } else { 'unknown' }\n$flag = Join-Path $dir $sid\n\nif ($Mode -eq 'probe') {\n  # ponytail: regex over the raw JSON instead of walking it - catches every\n  # used_percentage (5h window, 7d window, context window) whatever the shape.\n  $pcts = [regex]::Matches($raw, '\"used_percentage\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9.]+)') | ForEach-Object { [double]$_.Groups[1].Value }\n  if ($pcts) { Write-Flag $flag (($pcts | Measure-Object -Maximum).Maximum) }\n  # The weekly number needs its own file: the main flag is read by the sh twin\n  # as bare bytes with no line endings (A12), so a second value cannot share it.\n  # Greedy `[^}]*`, like the sh twin, so both pick the same used_percentage if\n  # seven_day ever carries more than one. NOT byte-identical behaviour: .NET's\n  # `\\s*` and `[^}]*` cross newlines, while the sh twin strips spaces and tabs\n  # then greps line by line - so a pretty-printed payload yields a number here\n  # and none there. Claude Code sends one line; if that ever changes, the sh\n  # twin is the one that breaks.\n  if ($raw -match '\"seven_day\"\\s*:\\s*\\{[^}]*\"used_percentage\"\\s*:\\s*([0-9.]+)') {\n    Write-Flag \"$flag.weekly\" $Matches[1]\n  } else {\n    Remove-Item \"$flag.weekly\" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue   # never let a stale weekly number outlive its payload\n  }\n  if ($Then) { $raw | & powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -Command $Then }\n  exit 0\n}\n\n# gate\nif ($raw -match '\"stop_hook_active\"\\s*:\\s*true') { exit 0 }   # never loop on ourselves\n# No early return on a missing main flag: the two flags are written\n# independently, so \"weekly recorded, max not\" is a reachable state, and it is\n# the one a broken probe leaves behind.\n$pct = Read-Flag $flag\n$wk = $null\n$done = \"$flag.done\"\n# A83: codex has no statusline, so no probe ever wrote a flag. Its Stop stdin\n# carries transcript_path, this session's own rollout - read the usage there.\n# Only a rollout-*.jsonl counts: Claude Code's Stop sends one too.\n# A98: window_minutes 10080 is the 7-day window - Get-CodexWeeklyPct reads it\n# separately so it gets the same weekly gate as the Claude path below.\nif ($null -eq $pct -and -not (Test-Path \"$flag.weekly\") -and\n    $raw -match '\"transcript_path\"\\s*:\\s*\"([^\"]+)\"') {\n  $tp = $Matches[1] -replace '\\\\\\\\', '/'   # JSON's escaped backslash pair\n  if ((Split-Path $tp -Leaf) -like 'rollout-*.jsonl' -and (Test-Path -LiteralPath $tp)) {\n    $pct = Get-CodexPct $tp\n    $wk  = Get-CodexWeeklyPct $tp\n  }\n}\n# Weekly first, and with its OWN one-shot marker. Sharing `$flag.done` meant a\n# 5-hour fire at 10:00 silently ate the weekly order when the 7-day window\n# crossed at 14:00 - the one window whose crossing the user has to answer.\nif ($null -eq $wk) { $wk = Read-Flag \"$flag.weekly\" }\nif ($null -ne $wk -and $wk -ge $wkLimit -and -not (Test-Path \"$flag.weekly.done\")) {\n  New-Item \"$flag.weekly.done\" -ItemType File -Force | Out-Null\n  @{ decision = 'block'; reason = (Get-WeeklyReason $wk $wkLimit) } | ConvertTo-Json -Compress\n  exit 0\n}\nif (Test-Path $done) { exit 0 }\nif ($null -eq $pct -or $pct -lt $pctLimit) { exit 0 }\nNew-Item $done -ItemType File -Force | Out-Null\n@{ decision = 'block'; reason = (Get-Reason $pct $pctLimit) } | ConvertTo-Json -Compress\n\r\n### C:\\Users\\Ikran\\AppData\\Local\\Temp\\claude\\C--tools-Projects-skillator2\\513fec64-069a-4a3e-839f-33d114b30f8f\\scratchpad\\a58d\\audit-sherlock-2\\.agents\\skills\\watch-cortana\\hooks\\usage-watch.sh\r\n#!/bin/sh\n# watch-cortana: record usage % from the statusline, act on it at the Stop hook.\n#   usage-watch.sh probe [\"<original statusline command>\"]   (statusLine command)\n#   usage-watch.sh gate                                      (Stop hook)\n#   usage-watch.sh check                                     (any host, no stdin)\n# Threshold: $CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_PCT, default 92.\nmode=\"${1:-probe}\"; then_cmd=\"$2\"\n# The state dir keeps its original name on purpose: it is storage, not a\n# skill reference. Renaming it with the skill would strand every live\n# session's flag and .done marker at the old path - a session that had\n# already fired would fire a second time. Skill renames must not migrate\n# a user's machine.\ndir=\"$HOME/.claude/handoff-watch\"\nlimit=\"${CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_PCT:-92}\"\n# The 7-day window is watched separately and lower. The 5-hour window refills in\n# hours, so crossing it is a pause; the weekly one does not, so crossing it ends\n# the week's work - which is why it gets its own threshold and its own fourth\n# step: ask where to go next rather than leave the user to guess.\nwk_limit=\"${CLAUDE_USAGE_HANDOFF_WEEKLY_PCT:-90}\"\nwk_step4=' (4) This is the 7-DAY window, which does not refill for days - the work is over for now, not paused. So after the handoff, do not stop on a summary: use AskUserQuestion to put the next direction to the user as concrete options drawn from the open board and the handoff'\"'\"'s own next-steps, say which one you recommend and why in one line, and make the recommendation the first option.'\n\n# The flag file is shared with the PowerShell twin, which used to write it with\n# a UTF-8 BOM and CRLF. A BOM makes awk string-compare (0xEF > '9'), so \"12.0\"\n# read as \">= 92\" and the one-shot handoff was burned at 12%. Take the first\n# numeric token in the file and nothing else: no BOM, CR or stray space survives.\nread_pct() {\n  [ -f \"$1\" ] || return 0\n  awk '{ if (match($0, /[0-9]+([.][0-9]+)?/)) { print substr($0, RSTART, RLENGTH); exit } }' \"$1\"\n}\n# Numeric compare, forced numeric on both sides.\nover_limit() { [ \"$(awk -v a=\"$1\" -v b=\"$2\" 'BEGIN{print (a+0 >= b+0)}')\" = 1 ]; }\n\n# A32: a rollout nobody has written to in this long is not this session's usage.\n# Must match $staleHours in the PowerShell twin.\nstale_secs=10800   # 3 hours\n\n# Every rollout under $1, at ANY depth, one path per line.\n# Deliberately NOT `find`: on Windows, sh launched from a non-MSYS parent\n# (PowerShell, the Claude Code host) inherits a PATH with System32 first, so\n# `find` is Windows' find.exe and answers \"File not found - rollout-*.jsonl\"\n# while exiting 0 - a silent empty result, which is exactly the failure this\n# ticket is about. Plain shell recursion has no PATH to lose. (`sort` is the\n# other name Windows steals, and `probe` used to pipe through it. That was\n# wrong: the watch-cortana selftest drives this script from PowerShell, where\n# `sort.exe` eats `-g` and prints \"The system cannot find the file specified\"\n# while exiting 0 - so `probe` wrote the weekly flag and no main flag at all.\n# Every numeric compare in this file now goes through awk.)\ncodex_walk() {\n  for p in \"$1\"/*; do\n    [ -e \"$p\" ] || continue          # unmatched glob comes back literal\n    if [ -d \"$p\" ]; then codex_walk \"$p\"\n    else case ${p##*/} in rollout-*.jsonl) printf '%s\n' \"$p\" ;; esac\n    fi\n  done\n}\n\n# \"<epoch> <path>\" per argument. POSIX defines no mtime primitive: `stat -c` is\n# GNU/busybox, `stat -f` is BSD/macOS, `date -r` means \"this file\" on GNU but\n# \"this many seconds\" on BSD. Try them in an order where the wrong one fails\n# rather than lies (GNU answers `stat -f` with '?', so -c must come first), and\n# take whole batches at once so this is one fork, not one per rollout.\nmtime_list() {\n  stat -c '%Y %n' \"$@\" 2>/dev/null && return 0\n  stat -f '%m %N' \"$@\" 2>/dev/null && return 0\n  for f in \"$@\"; do\n    e=$(date -r \"$f\" +%s 2>/dev/null)\n    case $e in ''|*[!0-9]*) continue ;; esac\n    printf '%s %s\n' \"$e\" \"$f\"\n  done\n}\n\n# `date +%s` is not POSIX either; awk's srand() returns the PREVIOUS seed, which\n# is the current epoch, and awk is required to exist.\nnow_epoch() {\n  n=$(date +%s 2>/dev/null)\n  case $n in ''|*[!0-9]*) n=$(awk 'BEGIN{srand();print srand()}') ;; esac\n  printf '%s' \"$n\"\n}\n\n# Newest rollout under $1, but ONLY if it was written inside the freshness\n# window - same rule, same window, as the PowerShell twin. Prints nothing when\n# there is none, when the newest is stale, or when this host offers no way to\n# read an mtime at all. That last case is a deliberate decline: a rollout we\n# cannot date is a rollout we must not fire on. Declining costs a printed\n# reminder; firing wrongly burns the one-shot .done and derails a live session.\ncodex_newest_fresh() {\n  [ -d \"$1\" ] || return 0\n  # Collect first, split second: `set -f` must not be in force while codex_walk\n  # runs, or the globbing it is built on is switched off under it.\n  list=$(codex_walk \"$1\")\n  [ -n \"$list\" ] || return 0\n  oIFS=$IFS\n  IFS='\n'\n  set -f                              # newline-split the list, but do not glob it\n  set -- $list\n  set +f\n  IFS=$oIFS\n  [ \"$#\" -gt 0 ] || return 0\n  mtime_list \"$@\" | awk -v now=\"$(now_epoch)\" -v w=\"$stale_secs\" '\n    { t = $1 + 0; sub(/^[^ ]* /, \"\")\n      if (t > m) { m = t; p = $0 } }\n    END { if (p != \"\" && now - m < w) print p }'\n}\n\n# Last token_count line of a codex rollout, or nothing.\ncodex_last_tc() { tail -400 \"$1\" | grep '\"token_count\"' | tail -1; }\n\n# Usage % from one codex rollout: the max of every rate_limits used_percent\n# whose window is NOT the 7-day one (A98: window_minutes 10080), plus the\n# live context (last_token_usage.total_tokens / model_context_window), on its\n# last token_count line. Prints nothing when there is no such line.\ncodex_pct() {\n  line=$(codex_last_tc \"$1\")\n  [ -n \"$line\" ] || return 0\n  # last_token_usage is the live context; total_token_usage is cumulative\n  # for the session and would read well over 100%.\n  printf '%s' \"$line\" | awk '\n    match($0,/\"last_token_usage\":\\{[^}]*\"total_tokens\":[0-9]+/){\n      t=substr($0,RSTART,RLENGTH); sub(/.*\"total_tokens\":/,\"\",t) }\n    match($0,/\"model_context_window\":[0-9]+/){\n      w=substr($0,RSTART,RLENGTH); sub(/.*:/,\"\",w) }\n    { m=0; s=$0\n      while (match(s,/\\{[^{}]*\"used_percent\":[0-9.]+[^{}]*\\}/)) {\n        block=substr(s,RSTART,RLENGTH)\n        if (block !~ /\"window_minutes\":10080/) {\n          v=block; sub(/.*\"used_percent\":/,\"\",v); sub(/[^0-9.].*/,\"\",v)\n          if (v+0>m) m=v+0 }\n        s=substr(s,RSTART+RLENGTH) } }\n    END { if (w+0>0 && t+0>0) { c=100*t/w; if (c>m) m=c }\n          if (m>0) printf \"%.1f\", m }'\n}\n\n# A98: the 10080-minute rate_limits window is codex's 7-day window, the\n# counterpart of the Claude path's seven_day.used_percentage - it gets the\n# weekly gate (90% hard stop, step-4 question) instead of the 92% one.\n# Prints nothing when the last token_count line has no such window.\ncodex_wk_pct() {\n  line=$(codex_last_tc \"$1\")\n  [ -n \"$line\" ] || return 0\n  printf '%s' \"$line\" | awk '\n    { wk=\"\"; s=$0\n      while (match(s,/\\{[^{}]*\"used_percent\":[0-9.]+[^{}]*\\}/)) {\n        block=substr(s,RSTART,RLENGTH)\n        if (block ~ /\"window_minutes\":10080/) {\n          v=block; sub(/.*\"used_percent\":/,\"\",v); sub(/[^0-9.].*/,\"\",v)\n          if (wk==\"\" || v+0>wk+0) wk=v }\n        s=substr(s,RSTART+RLENGTH) } }\n    END { if (wk!=\"\") printf \"%.1f\", wk+0 }'\n}\n\n# check: any host, no stdin. It is read below, by name, not by mtime.\n    f=$(ls -t \"$dir\" 2>/dev/null | grep -v '\\.done$' | grep -v '\\.weekly$' | head -1)\n    [ -n \"$f\" ] && { pct=$(read_pct \"$dir/$f\"); src=claude-code; key=$f; wk=$(read_pct \"$dir/$f.weekly\"); }\n    # A weekly flag with no main flag beside it is a real state (see the gate),\n    # so fall back to it rather than reporting no signal at all.\n    if [ -z \"$f\" ]; then\n      f=$(ls -t \"$dir\" 2>/dev/null | grep '\\.weekly$' | head -1)\n      [ -n \"$f\" ] && { wk=$(read_pct \"$dir/$f\"); src=claude-code; key=${f%.weekly}; }\n    fi\n  fi\n  if [ -z \"$pct\" ] && [ -z \"$wk\" ]; then\n    echo \"watch-cortana: no usage signal on this host - run skillator:handoff-cortana manually before you run out\"; exit 0\n  fi\n  # The weekly gate applies on every host, not just the one with a Stop hook.\n  # It has its own .done, so a 5-hour fire earlier in the session cannot eat\n  # the weekly order when the 7-day window crosses hours later.\n  if [ -n \"$wk\" ] && over_limit \"$wk\" \"$wk_limit\" && [ ! -f \"$dir/$key.weekly.done\" ]; then\n    mkdir -p \"$dir\"; : > \"$dir/$key.weekly.done\"\n    echo \"HANDOFF NOW ($src 7-day $wk%)\"\n    printf 'Usage has reached %s%% of the limit (threshold %s%%).
